## Project-wide admission triage and statement corrections (2026-10-20)

The ordinary build passed before this review with **1,356 admissions**, matching
a comment-stripped source census. Every admission was classified by area against the
proved project core, Mathlib and TauCeti. Each cited source lemma was checked to exist
and not to be admitted. The routes were reasoned out but not compiled, so the tier
counts below are estimates, not proofs.

The tiers are:

- **A**: a short proof from proved results (specialization, bridge, finite calculation).
- **B**: needs one new shared helper, which also unlocks other admissions.
- **C**: needs substantial new theory.
- **D**: defer (zero tables and counts, numerics, uniform and turning-point asymptotics,
  hyperasymptotics, continued-fraction convergence, Mehler–Fock inversion).
- **F**: suspected false, mis-transcribed or missing a hypothesis.

Totals: A 302, B 428, C 374, D 236, F 16. By area:

- Airy, 363: A 58, B 142, C 105, D 51, F 7.
- Legendre, 293: A 68, B 101, C 90, D 33, F 1.
- Incomplete Gamma/Beta and exponential integrals, 248: A 56, B 95, C 43, D 54, F 0.
- Error functions and parabolic cylinders, 218: A 66, B 43, C 48, D 59, F 2.
- Struve, Anger–Weber, Lommel and Bessel, 119: A 43, B 26, C 30, D 16, F 4.
- Earlier theory, 115: A 1 plus 10 Whittaker corollaries of admitted Kummer
  representations, B 21, C 58, D 23, F 2.

No earlier-theory admission is used by any proof elsewhere in the project, so none of
them blocks a newer proof directly. Their value is mathematical reuse.

### Statement corrections (Priority 0)

Each correction was compared with the DLMF TeX source on 2026-10-20 and is recorded
in the declaration docstring.

- `besselJ_third_eq_airy_complex` (9.6.11): the bracket is now
  `sqrt 3 * Ai(-z) - ε * Bi(-z)`, as in DLMF. The earlier transcription had the bracket
  of 9.6.13 and was false at `z = 1`.
- 9.6.11, 9.6.13, 9.6.15, 9.6.17 and 9.6.19: the factor `(3/z)^(1/2)` is now
  `sqrt 3 * z^(-1/2)`. The left sides use the logarithmic `airyLogZeta`; on the negative
  real axis the principal `(3/z)^(1/2)` has the opposite sign, so the hypothesis `z ≠ 0`
  made each statement false at `z = -1`.
- `smirnovAiryU1_olver`, `smirnovAiryU2_olver` (9.13.23–24): `Even m` added. DLMF
  defines `W_m(±t)` only for even `m` (9.13.17); for odd `m` the order-`t^m` term does
  not cancel.
- `weberM1` (11.10.24): now `(n+1)/2`, the docstring's own `⌊(n-1)/2⌋+1`. The earlier
  `n/2` gave an empty sum at `n = 1`, contradicting `weberE_zero`.
- `struveH_relative_bound` (11.4.14–15): the bound is now
  `2/3 * (exp(|z|^2/(4d)) - 1)`. **DLMF 11.4.15 as printed,
  `2/3 * exp(|z|^2/(4d) - 1)`, is false**: at `ν = 1000`, `z = iy`, `y^2/4 = |ν+3/2|`
  the series gives `|θ| > 0.93`. The corrected bound follows from
  `(3/2)_n ≥ (3/2) n!` and `|(ν+3/2)_n| ≥ d^n`.
- `struveH_bessel_series_18` (11.4.18): hypothesis `ν + 1/2 ∉ -ℕ` added. DLMF's term
  with `2k+2ν+1 = 0` is a removable quotient, but the totalized Lean term is zero, so the
  statement claimed `H_{-1/2} = 0`.
- `struveH_integral_product` (11.7.12): hypotheses `Re μ, Re ν > -3/2` added. DLMF states
  only `Re(μ+ν) > 0`, under which the integral diverges (at `μ = 3`, `ν = -2` the
  integrand grows like `t^(1/2) cos(...)`).
- `pcfOlverPair` (12.10.11): the coefficient of the `u`-equation is now `3(s+1)`, as in
  DLMF. With `3/2(s+1)` no polynomial solution exists at `s = 0`, so `Classical.epsilon`
  returned an unspecified polynomial and `pcfOlverU_spec`, `pcfOlverUV_initial` were false.
  The `r`-recurrence coefficient `3/2(s+1)` (12.10.12) was already correct.
- `conicalEta_mapping` (14.20(viii)): hypothesis now `0 < α`. At `α = 0` the relation is
  `sqrt η = arccos x`, so `η → π^2`, not `∞`, as `x → -1`.
- 13.9.11–15, zeros of `U(a,b,·)`: DLMF's `T(a,b)` counts zeros in the sector `|ph z| < π`
  under `b ≤ 1` and nonintegral `a`, `b`, `a-b+1`. Its `P(a,b)` counts **positive** zeros.
  - The old `kummerU_posZeros_gamma_pos/neg` used the `T` formulas for positive zeros,
    without hypotheses, and were false (for example `(a,b) = (-1.3,-2.2)` has no positive
    zeros). They are replaced by `kummerU_sectorZeros_gamma_pos/neg` over the new
    `kummerUSectorZeros`, with DLMF's hypotheses.
  - 13.9.14 is acquired as `kummerU_posZeros_ceil` (admitted).
  - 13.9.15 is **proved** as `kummerU_posZeros_none_of_le`, stronger than DLMF. Kummer's
    transformation reduces it to the proved 13.9.13 form at first parameter `a-b+1 ≥ 0`.
  - The earlier `kummerU_negZeros_none` concerns the negative axis, so its `dlmf 13.9.E15`
    tag was removed. The statement is unchanged. The earlier removal of a negative-axis
    "13.9.14" rested on the same misreading of `P`.

Also noted, but not changed: `expIntAuxF_laplace_integral` at `z = 0` relies on Lean's
`log 0 = 0`. `legendreQPoly_convolution` uses `legendreP` at all complex `z`, where the
polynomial bridge is so far proved only for `1+z` in the slit plane.

### Priority 1: tier-A proofs, by area

1. Parabolic cylinders, about 47: all of `Basic` (rotated connections, real forms, phases,
   series), eleven `Relations` (Kummer/Whittaker forms, addition theorems, the Bessel-K
   values from the seed `pcfU_bessel_10`), both Hermite statements, twelve Weber-`W`
   results, and a few `Integrals`/`AsymptoticsZeros` items.
2. Legendre, about 68: `SpecialValues` (22), integer-order and Rodrigues results,
   reflections, singular behaviour, conical ODE and series, Christoffel sums, generating
   function, spherical-harmonic specializations, and boundary values from one-sided
   logarithm limits.
3. Incomplete Gamma and exponential integrals, about 56: Euler and Tricomi–Laplace
   integrals, the `E_p` series family, ODEs, entire dependence on the parameter, Ein and
   Ei real integrals, and Si/Cin auxiliary derivatives.
4. Struve, Anger and Weber, about 43: finite-interval calculus for Anger and Weber
   (entireness, derivatives, recurrences, ODEs, Anger–Weber relations), the `angerS1/S2`
   regularized series, parameter entireness of the Struve cores, and `lommelCapital_neg_order`.
5. Airy, about 58: twenty `airyChi` table rows from one closed form, the modulus/phase
   identities, negative-order Hankel unfoldings, double integrals, `airyU_product`,
   `scorer_zero`, and real zeros of the derivatives.
6. Error functions, about 19: complex erf and Faddeeva series, Fresnel tail integrals,
   three Voigt identities, Mills ratio, simple zeros.
7. Earlier theory: `mellin_regularizedHGFun` (Kummer transformation plus the proved
   `mellin_regularizedHGFun_exp`).

### Tier-A proof batch: results

Six parallel proof passes, one per area, and one direct proof removed **319 original
admissions**: from 1,356 to 1,038, counting the new 13.9.14 admission. No theorem
statement, hypothesis or definition was changed beyond the corrections listed above.
Some declarations were moved earlier within their files, unchanged, to respect the
dependency order. All helpers are proved, and no new Lean files were created.

- Airy, 98 (363 → 265):
  - `Relations`: 45 of 59, which includes all of 9.6.2–9.6.20 and therefore the five
    corrected relations. The proofs use logarithmic-plane Bessel lemmas built through
    `airyBesselIOnLog_airyLogZeta` and `airyBesselJOnLog_airyLogZeta`.
  - The twenty `airyChi` table rows, from closed forms for `χ(2m)` and `χ(2m+1)`.
  - Modulus and phase identities, ODEs and monotonicity.
  - Generalized Airy and Olver normalizations, Reid contour identities, incomplete-Airy
    derivatives, double integrals, Scorer values, and the real zeros of the derivatives.
  - The 14 Whittaker/Kummer forms (9.6.21 onward) remain admitted.
- Legendre, 66 (293 → 227): integer-order and Rodrigues results (`IntegerTheory` is
  now complete), 30 special values, `Singularities`, conical ODE and series, spherical
  harmonics including the addition theorem, a Christoffel sum, the generating function,
  boundary values, and Neumann integrals.
- Parabolic cylinders, 48 (106 → 58): all of `Basic` and `Hermite`, eleven `Relations`
  and twelve Weber-`W` results, three zero results, Temme polynomials, parameter
  scalings, and `pcfOlverUV_initial`. The last confirms the corrected `pcfOlverPair`
  against DLMF 12.10.9–10. The general `pcfOlverU_spec` remains admitted.
- Incomplete Gamma/Beta, 45 (179 → 134):
  - Euler and Laplace integrals, ODEs, entire parameter dependence and the residue.
  - The `E_p` series, Stieltjes and Mellin–Laplace forms.
  - The incomplete-Beta quadratic formula, Beta auxiliary recurrences, Padé and θ
    identities, and the large-parameter lower-Gamma expansion.
  - `genExpIntEExtended_zero` remains, because it depends on the admitted
    `isBigO_kummerU_at_zero_re_nonpos`.
- Struve, Anger and Weber, 32 (117 → 85): the Anger–Weber finite-integral calculus
  (entireness, derivatives, recurrences, ODEs, order continuation, Bessel limits),
  `angerS1/S2` series, Struve-core order entireness, `lommelCapital_neg_order`, and
  coefficient initial values.
- Error functions, 19 (112 → 93): complex erf and Faddeeva series, horizontal erfc and
  Fresnel integrals, the Goodwin–Staton integrability, Voigt identities, Mills ratio,
  and zero orders.
- Exponential integrals, 10 (69 → 59): auxiliary derivatives, the Ei principal value
  and series, the E₁ remainder sign, real Ein integrals, and the Acton recurrence.
- Confluent, 1: `mellin_regularizedHGFun` (13.10.10) by Kummer's transformation.
  Separately, 13.9.15 is newly proved as `kummerU_posZeros_none_of_le`. The two restated
  13.9.11–12 admissions replace the old pair one for one, and 13.9.14 adds one admission,
  so the Confluent total is unchanged at 49.

Current counts: Airy 265, Legendre 227, incomplete Gamma/Beta 134, error functions 93,
Struve 85, exponential integrals 59, cylinders 58, Confluent 49, orthogonal polynomials
39, Hypergeometric 14, spherical harmonics 4, Partitions 3, Selberg 3, Gamma 2,
Bessel 2, q-series 1.

The remaining tier-A items are mostly those that a pass judged heavier than expected.
They include `sphericalHarmonic_rodrigues` (negative order), `legendre_christoffel_7`
and the Ferrers-Q–dependent statements; these move to the B-tier helper work.

## Shared-helper round (Priority 2, 2026-10-20)

Seven parallel passes built the shared helpers ranked in the triage above, and a small
follow-up harvested their downstream consequences. They removed **279 admissions**, from
1,038 to 759, and introduced no new admission. No theorem statement, hypothesis or
definition was changed. Some declarations were moved unchanged to files where their
prerequisites are available: `ferrers_wronskian_5` and `associated_wronskian_11` moved to
`AssociatedRecurrences`, and Tricomi's Gauss/Stieltjes pair moved to `Confluent/Integrals`.

### New shared infrastructure

- [ParametricHolomorphy.lean](DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Complex/ParametricHolomorphy.lean)
  (Mathlib only):
  - holomorphy of locally dominated series and of interval, `Ioi` and general integrals in
    a finite-dimensional complex parameter;
  - a continuous Osgood lemma (separately holomorphic and continuous implies jointly
    holomorphic), proved from the one-variable Cauchy formula.
- [ParameterHolomorphy.lean](DLMF/Hypergeometric/ParameterHolomorphy.lean): **joint
  holomorphy of the continued regularized Gauss function in `(a,b,c,z)` on the cut plane**.
  The shift counts of the Euler-integral recursion are fixed on open parameter sets and
  then identified with `regularizedGaussHG`. This closes `entire_regularized_parameters`
  and supplies entire degree and order dependence for Legendre P and Olver Q.
- [Poisson.lean](DLMF/Struve/Poisson.lean): Poisson integrals for H, L and M.
- In-file helper families:
  - the Ferrers even/odd hypergeometric basis with its Wronskian;
  - Ferrers functions continued into the unit disk, with side boundary values;
  - the logarithmic-plane upper-Gamma package (difference from Γ, `a = 0` value, recurrence);
  - integration along rays using Tricomi decay;
  - the erfc bound for `Re z^2 > 0`;
  - the Voigt Fubini representation;
  - Reid contour calculus with the finite part;
  - the generalized-Airy derivative system and the Scorer `Gi` series;
  - Anger sine-power moments.

- Airy, 105 (265 → 160):
  - `Relations` and `Incomplete` are complete.

### Newly found statement defects (left admitted, decision needed)

- `reidAiry_connection_36` (DLMF 9.13.36) is **false at `p = 1`** with the project's
  finite-part definition of `reidAiryB`. The failure is proved in Lean as
  `reidAiry_connection_36_ne_one`. The logarithmic term `θ i c_{p-1}` uses `θ = 0` for B₀,
  while the contour of A₁ starts at angle 2π, so B₂ − B₀ = 2πi·A₁ + 2πi·B₀(z,1) with
  B₀(z,1) = 1. Either the finite-part convention or the statement must be adjusted.
- `conicalQHat_boundary` (DLMF 14.23.7): the earlier note here was wrong about the cause.
  The project's `legendreBoundary` and `associatedQ` already agree with DLMF 14.23.1–14.23.6
  (DLMF prints P(x ± i0) = e^{∓μπi/2} 𝖯, the same as the project); this was rechecked with
  mpmath, independently of the project code. The defect is in DLMF 14.23.7 itself: with
  DLMF's definitions 14.20.2 and 14.23.2, the second factor must be e^{μπi/2}, not
  e^{-3μπi/2}. With the correction the two terms are complex conjugates and the sum is
  real, as Q̂ is; the printed form is not real. The statement is corrected (2026-10-20, at
  the user's direction to follow DLMF conventions) and now proved, without the unused
  assumptions τ ≥ 0, μ ≥ 0 (stronger than DLMF).
- Further analysis of `reidAiry_connection_36`: the defect is in DLMF, not in a project
  convention. For integer p, 9.13.34 gives A₁ + A₂ + A₃ = −B₀(z,p), so adding 9.13.35–9.13.37
  gives 0 = −2πi·B₀(z,p) whatever the definition of B_k. Since B₀(z,p) is the residue
  c_{p−1}(z) of t^{−p}e^{zt−t³/3} at t = 0 (B₀(z,1) = 1), the three relations are mutually
  inconsistent for every p ≥ 1 with c_{p−1}(z) ≠ 0. They hold for p ≤ 0, the only range in
  which the ray integrals 9.13.27 converge. The regularization of B_k only decides which
  of the three relations fails; with the project's choice, 9.13.35 and 9.13.37 hold for all
  p and 9.13.36 becomes B₃ − B₁ = 2πi(A₂ + B₀(z,p)) (DLMF numbering).
  Resolved at the user's direction: `reidAiry_connection_36` (dlmf tag) now states and
  proves the corrected form for all integer p, and `reidAiry_connection_36_of_nonpos` proves
  DLMF's printed form for p ≤ 0, using the new lemma `reidAiryB0_of_nonpos`
  (B₀(z,p) = 0 for p ≤ 0).

## Blocker round (2026-10-20, late evening)

This round attacked the six blockers listed above, one parallel agent per blocker, with
file-level ownership. It took the project from 757 to **692** admissions (65 proved), with
no new admission and no statement change. One more statement was found to be false and is
left admitted for a decision.

### New infrastructure

- `DLMF/Bessel/KIntegralRep.lean`: DLMF 10.32.9 (`besselK_eq_integral_cosh`, all ν,
  ℜ z > 0) and 10.32.10 (`besselK_eq_integral_schlafli_of_abs_arg_lt`, |ph z| < π/4),
  order-uniform bounds `norm_besselK_le`, `norm_besselK_le_exp`, and holomorphy of K_ν
  and I_ν on the slit plane.
- `DLMF/Bessel/YIntegralRep.lean`: the Struve K Laplace integral 11.5.2 for all ν
  (`struveK_eq_integral`), DLMF 10.9.5 (`besselY_eq_integral`), Poisson's integral for
  complex z, `hankelH1_eq_integral_Ioi_one`, and the K recurrence. It imports
  `DLMF.Struve.Properties`.
- `DLMF/Bessel/YSchlafli.lean`: DLMF 10.9.7 (`besselY_eq_schlafli`), all ν, ℜ z > 0.
- `DLMF/Bessel/Schlafli.lean`: DLMF 10.9.6 (`besselJ_eq_schlafli`), complex ν, ℜ z > 0, via
  Hankel's 1/Γ formula in exponential coordinates (`schlafli_gamma`), plus a boundary form
  for ℜ z = 0.
- `DLMF/Bessel/ProductIntegral.lean`: DLMF 10.9.26 (`besselJ_mul_besselJ_eq_integral`) and
  DLMF 5.12.5 for complex parameters (`integral_cos_cpow_mul_cos`).
- `DLMF/Bessel/NeumannSeries.lean`: interchange of Neumann series with power series for
  exponents 2k and k (J and I), holomorphy, Neumann's expansion of (z/2)^l, a fibre
  regrouping lemma, regularized 13.6.9 for all c, and two terminating identities proved by
  WZ certificates.
- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/ODE/PowerSeriesUniqueness.lean` (Mathlib only): Taylor recurrence
  and its converse for second-order linear ODEs, analytic uniqueness (local and on
  preconnected open sets), Gronwall uniqueness on intervals and the line, and existence
  and uniqueness for 2×2 linear systems on compact intervals.
- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Asymptotics/WatsonRotated.lean` (Mathlib only): rotation of Laplace
  rays, Watson's lemma along rotated rays, and gluing of expansions on overlapping
  sectors.

All eight files are in the umbrellas (`DLMF/Bessel.lean`, `DLMFMathlib.lean`).

### Statement defect found in this round (resolved 2026-10-21)

- `regularizedHGFun_besselJ_series` (DLMF 13.11.3) was false as stated with principal
  branches. Its hypotheses allowed ph z + ph(b − 2a) > π, and then the factors
  (b−2a)^{(1−b−s)/2}, (z/2)^{(1−b+s)/2} and J_{b−1+s}(√(2z(b−2a))) use mismatched branches.
  At a = 0.2 − 0.5i, b = 1.4 + 0.4i, z = −1.2 + 0.3i the stated right side is
  2.8336 − 3.3899i, while M̃(a,b,z) = 1.18529 + 0.46781i (rechecked with mpmath).
- Resolution, at the user's request: the branch-free form
  `regularizedHGFun_eq_besselExpansion` (dlmf tag, stronger than DLMF) states
  M̃(a,b,z) = e^{z/2} Σ A_s (z/2)^s ₀F̃₁(; b+s; −(b−2a)z/2) for all complex a, b, z, with no
  hypotheses. The printed Bessel form `regularizedHGFun_besselJ_series` now assumes
  z ≠ 0, b − 2a ≠ 0 and ph z + ph(b − 2a) ∈ (−π, π], and follows term by term.
- Proof (original): the recurrence 13.11.4 for A_s is the formal equation
  (1−t²)A′ = (bt + (2a−b)t²)A, so Σ A_s t^s = e^{(b−2a)t}(1−t)^{−a}(1+t)^{a−b}. The coefficients
  B_N = Σ_{m+r=N} (a)_m 2^m (−1)^r (b+m)_r/(m! r!) of the last two factors satisfy a three-term
  recurrence, proved by telescoping with the certificate G(j+1) = −2(a+j)·term(j, N+1−j).
  Regrouping the double series by total degree, and using (b+m)_r/Γ(b+m+r) = 1/Γ(b+m) (valid at
  the poles), gives the Cauchy product of the M̃ series with e^{−z/2}.

  oscillatory J_ν integral machinery.
- Continuation of U(a, z) past |ph z| = π/2 (connection formulas or a log-plane Tricomi U)

- The cubic transformation behind 15.8.32 `cubic_connection` (true numerically).
- A K_μK_ν product formula for Nicholson's integral; Gegenbauer's addition theorem.
- Conditionally convergent Struve integrals (11.5.7, 11.7.9–12) and Barnes integrals
  (11.5.8–9); the Struve-primitive and Anger–Weber large-parameter clusters.
- `fresnelAux_laplace_open` gives 7.7.10–11 on the open sector; the closed-sector

## Reciprocal-Gamma strengthening (2026-10-21)

Mathlib's `Complex.digamma` is `logDeriv Gamma`, which is `0` at the poles `0, −1, −2, …`. A
product ψ(x)·(1/Γ(x)) therefore evaluates to `0` there, although its continuous extension is
`(−1)^{n+1} n!` at `x = −n`. A census found eleven declarations whose pole-avoiding hypotheses
existed only for this reason. The new module `DLMF/Gamma/RecipGammaDeriv.lean` provides the
entire functions `recipGammaDeriv n = (1/Γ)^{(n)}` with `(1/Γ)' = −ψ/Γ`, `(1/Γ)'(−p) = (−1)^p p!`,
`(1/Γ)'' = (ψ² − ψ')/Γ`, and the pole-free `poch_mul_recip_Gamma_add_nat`.

New theorems, each valid for all parameters and tagged as the original ("Stronger than DLMF"):

- Legendre (`ParameterLimits.lean`): closed forms `legendreSinDigamma_eq` and
  `legendreSinSqTrigamma_eq`; the admissions `legendreSinDigamma_limit` and
  `legendreSinSqTrigamma_limit` are now proved. `Singularities.lean`: the new admitted
  `olverQ_log_singular_recipGamma` (14.8.9, all ν), from which the old `olverQ_log_singular` is
  now proved (net zero).
- Confluent: `tricomi_numerator_nat_series_recipGamma`, `deriv_tricomi_numerator_nat_recipGamma`
  (`ParameterDerivatives.lean`); the series `tricomiLogSeriesRecipGamma` and
  `tricomi_log_series_split_recipGamma` (`LogSeries.lean`); `kummerU_nat_log_recipGamma`,
  `kummerU_nat_denom_recipGamma` (13.2.9, all a) and `isBigO_kummerU_at_zero_b_one_recipGamma`
  (13.2.19) in `BasicProperties.lean`.
- Hypergeometric (new file `LogConnectionsRecipGamma.lean`): 15.8.10
  `logarithmic_connection_one_sub_recipGamma`, 15.8.9
  `logarithmic_connection_one_div_one_sub_recipGamma` and 15.8.11
  `logarithmic_connection_one_sub_recip_recipGamma`, with all integrality and pole hypotheses
  on a, b, c − a dropped; helpers `recip_Gamma_mul_deriv_poch`, `recip_Gamma_mul_poch_one_sub`,
  `recip_Gamma_mul_deriv_poch_one_sub`.

The old statements are kept unchanged. All new statements were checked with mpmath at
parameter values the old hypotheses excluded (agreement to 1e-19 or better).

## Infrastructure round (round 5, 2026-10-21)

Six parallel agents with file-level ownership; 621 → 590 (31 admissions proved, none added,
no statement changed, no false statement found).

New general infrastructure (ToMathlib, Mathlib imports only, all fully proved):

- `Asymptotics/AsymptoticInversion.lean`: asymptotic power series `HasAsymptoticPowerSeries`
  with its algebra and composition (`comp`, `analytic_comp`), Lagrange inversion
  `HasAsymptoticPowerSeries.inversion` via Mathlib's compositional inverse
  `PowerSeries.substInvOfIsUnit`, and the McMahon form `HasAsymptoticPowerSeries.mcMahon` for
  solutions of ξ + G(ξ) = T along any filter (so for zero sequences k ↦ x_k).
- `Integral/Improper.lean`: Abel's theorem for improper integrals
  (`tendsto_abel_of_tendsto_intervalIntegral`), dominated convergence with Fubini for improper
  parametric integrals, and ∫₀^X u^{μ−1} sin u → Γ(μ) sin(πμ/2).
- `ODE/LiouvilleGreenVolterra.lean`, `LiouvilleGreen.lean`, `LiouvilleGreenReal.lean`,
  `LiouvilleGreenExpansion.lean`, `LiouvilleGreenComparison.lean` (namespace `LiouvilleGreen`):
  Volterra existence with the exp(∫ρ) − 1 bound (Olver Ch. 6, Thm 10.1); Olver's error bounds
  for w'' = (±u²f + g)w (Ch. 6, Thms 2.1–2.2, and the O(1/u) form of Ch. 10); asymptotic
  expansions with explicit error bounds (Ch. 10, Thm 3.1); a general comparison theorem and
  constant Wronskians as the base for turning-point theory (Ch. 11). All solutions are
  controlled from +∞; finite-endpoint control and dominant solutions are not yet covered.

Proved targets:

- Airy: `exists_airy_zero_parametrizations` (9.9.6–9.9.17).
- Parabolic cylinder (`SectorAsymptotics.lean`, new): rotated Laplace representation of U for
  |θ| < π/4, the expansion 12.9.1 on |ph z| ≤ 3π/4 − δ for every complex a, its log-plane form,
  and from the connection formulas 12.2.19–20 the targets `pcfU_sector_asymptotic`,
  `pcfV_sector_asymptotic`, `pcfU/V_adjacent_asymptotic_pos/neg` (12.9.1–12.9.4) and
  `pcfWS_asymptotic` (12.14.20–23).
- Parabolic cylinder uniform (`Uniform.lean`, `UniformLG.lean` new): `pcfOlverU_spec` (12.10.11),
  `pcfGammaCoeff_expansion` and `pcfGammaCoeff_initial` (12.10.16–17); and the new
  `pcf_negative_parameter_uniform_U` (U part of 12.10.3 with an existential normalizer, by
  Liouville–Green with u = μ², f = t² − 1, identified with U by a recessive-solution Wronskian
  argument and the large-z limit from 12.9.1).
- Struve: `struveL_fourier` (11.5.7, by Abel regularization and Laplace kernels),
  `struveH_integral_plain`, `struveH_integral_mellin_zero`, `struveH_integral_mellin`
  (11.7.9–11.7.11).
- Legendre (`IntegralTools.lean` new): `ferrers_orthogonality_6`–`_9` (14.17.6–9),
  `ferrersP_fractional_integral`, `associatedP_fractional_integral` (14.12.2, 14.12.5),
  the five negative-order integrals 14.6.6–8, `associatedP_fourier_integral` (14.12.7, all ν),
  `associatedP_nat_integral`, `olverQ_nat_finite_integral`, `olverQ_nat_interval_integral`,
  `olverQ_nat_heine` (14.12.8, 14.12.9, 14.12.11, 14.12.14).

Remaining blockers identified by the agents:

- Zero existence and localization (Rouché or argument-principle counts in sectors) for the
  erf/Fresnel, trigonometric-integral, Airy complex and Kummer zero expansions; the 7.13
  expansions also need inversion with log-polynomial coefficients.
- The modulus–phase expansions 9.8.20–23, after which the real Airy zero expansions follow
  from `mcMahon`.
- A decay bound 𝐌(a, b, −t) = O(t^{−Re a}) as t → +∞, which blocks all of
  `Confluent/Integrals.lean` and 13.4.6; 13.10.13–15 and 13.23.9–11 also need

- Liouville–Green control from a finite endpoint (V parts of 12.10.3–6, the oscillatory range

## Upstream pointers (2026-10-21)

The free-text notes recording DLMF results already in Mathlib or TauCeti are now 178 checked
`dlmf_upstream` pointers (`DLMF/Core/Upstream.lean`) in the `MathlibCoverage.lean` files of the
DLMF, Gamma, Confluent, Hypergeometric, Bessel, Legendre, ParabolicCylinder, Elliptic and
OrthogonalPolynomials areas and in `DLMF/Core/UpstreamElementary.lean` (Chapters 4, 5, 24, 25).
Each was compared with the live DLMF page (version 1.2.8) and the upstream statement, and
carries a relation keyword. The comparison found three printed statements that fail at an edge
case; the pointers record the upstream form as a special case:

- 4.8.2, ln(z₁z₂) = ln z₁ + ln z₂ for −π ≤ ph z₁ + ph z₂ ≤ π, fails at ph z₁ + ph z₂ = −π
  (z₁ = z₂ = −i gives iπ on the left and −iπ on the right).
- 4.8.9, ln(exp z) = z for −π ≤ Im z ≤ π, fails at Im z = −π (z = −iπ gives iπ).
- 24.4.7 at n = 0: with the sum printed from k = 1 the left side is m and the right side m + 1;
  Mathlib's form sums from k = 0 with 0⁰ = 1 and holds for n = 0.

The old note for `Polynomial.Chebyshev.abs_eval_T_real_eq_one_iff` under Table 18.6.1 was not
converted, because that table does not contain the result. No upstream declaration named in
the old notes had been renamed or removed.

Validation: the full build passes (9,891 jobs) with 590 `sorry` warnings, as before.

### Statements false as formalized (corrected 2026-10-21)

- `weberE_large_argument` (11.11.3): the finite sum bound only the F_k term; the `k` in the
  G_k term was an auto-bound implicit variable, so the statement quantified over it.
  Corrected (with the user's agreement) by bracketing the whole summand; the corrected
  statement is proved (by the agent's `weberE_large_argument_sum`, now under the original
  name). Net admissions after the correction: 508.
- `weberE_large_order` (11.11.6): DLMF's error term O(ν⁻²) is wrong for complex ν, because
  the remainder inherits the size e^{π|Im ν|} of sin²(πν/2) and cos²(πν/2). At z = 1,
  |error|·|ν|² is about 0.58 at ν = 10.5 but 1.05·10¹³ at ν = 1/2 + 10i and 9.4·10⁵³ at
  ν = 1/2 + 40i; divided by e^{π|Im ν|} it stays between 0.24 and 0.58. Corrected (with the
  user's agreement) to the error bound e^{π|Im ν|}ν⁻² on the same filter, which is DLMF's form on
  the real axis. Still admitted.
- `angerJ_turning_equiv` (11.11.18) and `weberE_turning_equiv` (11.11.19): DLMF 1.1.2 extended
  the condition ν → +∞ to ν → ∞ in |ph ν| ≤ π − δ ("Clarification", suggested 2021-04-05); the
  extension fails. By 11.10.15, 𝐉_ν(ν) = J_ν(ν) + sin(πν)𝐀_ν(ν) with 𝐀_ν(ν) ~ 1/(2πν), and the
  second term grows like e^{π|Im ν|}/|ν|. Numerically (Gauss–Legendre on the defining
  integrals), the ratio of 𝐉_ν(ν) to the claimed leading term is 0.9997, 0.9999, 1.0000 at
  ν = 20, 40, 80, but about 2.8·10⁶, 2.0·10¹⁴, 1.7·10³⁰ in modulus at the same |ν| with
  ph ν = 0.3, matching sin(πν)𝐀_ν(ν) to three digits; 𝐄_ν(ν) behaves the same way through
  cos(πν)𝐀_ν(ν) (11.10.16). Corrected (with the user's agreement) to real ν → +∞, the original
  DLMF condition. Still admitted; the proof needs the Bessel transition asymptotics 10.19.8.
- `angerA_turning_equiv` (11.11.16): verified numerically after the round (ratio 0.992, 0.995,
  0.997 at |ν| = 20, 40, 80, on the real axis and at ph ν = ±0.3). It is not false.

These three DLMF errata (11.11.6 error term, 11.11.18 and 11.11.19 sector) are candidates for a
report to the DLMF editors.

### autoImplicit switched off (2026-10-21)

Lean's defaults `autoImplicit = true` and `relaxedAutoImplicit = true` let an undeclared name
in a statement become an extra implicit variable. An audit compiled each of the 349 files that
did not already set `autoImplicit false` with both options off, subtracting baseline errors:
the only declaration that depended on an auto-bound name was `weberE_large_argument`
(11.11.3), corrected above. Both options are now `false` under `[leanOptions]` in
`lakefile.toml`, as in Mathlib. Note that `lake env lean FILE` does not apply package options;
to reproduce the build setting by hand use `lake env lean -DautoImplicit=false
-DrelaxedAutoImplicit=false FILE`.

## Round 7 statement corrections (2026-10-21, user-approved)

DLMF errata found by the round-7 agents and corrected in the formal statements:

- DLMF 9.10.7 (`airyBi_negative_integral_asymptotic`, `DLMF/Airy/Integrals.lean`): the leading
  term has the wrong sign. The correct form is ∫_{−∞}^x Bi(t) dt =
  −π^{−1/2}(−x)^{−3/4} sin(⅔(−x)^{3/2} + π/4) + O(|x|^{−9/4}) as x → −∞. At x = −10 the
  integral is −0.01504 while the printed formula gives +0.01240; at x = −40 the values are
  +0.007365 and −0.007249. Independently, by 9.7.11 the derivative of the printed leading term is
  −Bi(x), while the same check for 9.10.6 gives +Ai(x). The statement now has the minus sign and
  is proved.
- DLMF 13.9.6 (`kummerM_posZeros_ceil_swap`, `DLMF/Confluent/Zeros.lean`): DLMF states the count
  of positive zeros for all real a, b with ⌈−b⌉ > ⌈−a⌉ > 0, but it fails when a is a nonpositive
  integer, where M(a, b, ·) is a polynomial: at a = −1, b = −3/2, M = 1 + 2x/3 has no positive
  zero while the formula gives one (proved as `kummerM_posZeros_ceil_swap_ne`; mpmath shows the
  same at (−2, −4.5)). The statement now assumes a ∉ −ℕ and is proved.

Transcription correction (not a DLMF erratum):

- DLMF 13.17.1 and 13.17.3 (`whittakerM_continued_fraction_limit`,
  `whittakerW_continued_fraction_limit`, `DLMF/Confluent/ContinuedFractions.lean`): DLMF's
  equations are identities of meromorphic functions. At a zero of the denominator the convergents
  diverge, while Lean's division by zero gives 0 (μ = 0.5, κ = 12.3, x ≈ 0.5615 for 13.17.1;
  μ = 0.7, κ = 6.3, x ≈ 0.4755 for 13.17.3). Both statements now assume the denominator is
  nonzero.

Together with 11.11.6, 11.11.18 and 11.11.19 (round 6), the DLMF errata 9.10.7, 13.9.6 and (below)
14.14.1 are candidates for a report to the DLMF editors.

- `DLMFMathlib/Topology/Order/ZeroEnumeration.lean`: unique zeros of strictly monotone functions
  and the enumeration of zeros separated by a sequence of points.
- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/ContinuedFraction/Convergence.lean`: generalized continued fractions
  through three-term recurrences: determinant and exact error formulas, Pincherle's theorem, a
  ratio criterion (complex and real), bracketing for positive elements, a positive S-fraction
  criterion, and tails (a fraction converges when a far tail is controlled).
- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Asymptotics/StationaryPhase.lean`: oscillatory integrals with a cubic
  stationary point at an endpoint.
- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Complex/BarnesResidue.lean`: shifting vertical-line integrals across simple
  poles and across pole-free strips, with sine and Gamma bounds for Barnes integrands.
- DLMF: `ErrorFunctions/ZeroTools.lean` (Rouché against an exponential model, using TauCeti's
  Rouché theorem), `ErrorFunctions/IntegralTools.lean`, `IncompleteGamma/FractionTools.lean`,
  `Struve/TurningTools.lean`, `Legendre/SeriesTools.lean`, `ParabolicCylinder/IntegralTools.lean`,
  `Airy/ModulusTools.lean`, `Airy/IntegralTools.lean`.

Main results proved:

- Airy: 9.8.20–23 and the error bounds for M and N, all monotonicity results of §9.8(iii) (via
  Nicholson's integral), 9.10.2–7, 9.10.11–16, 9.11.4, 9.12.25–31, and infinitely many complex
  zeros of Bi and Bi′.
- Legendre: 14.7.3, 14.7.4, 14.7.19–22, 14.11.1–5, 14.12.12, 14.17.11–15 and 14.17.18–20 (the
  diagonal cases through parameter-uniform boundary bounds and dominated convergence),
  `ferrersP_sq_integral_all_degrees`, 14.19.2, 14.19.3, 14.19.7, 14.19.8, 14.20.7, 14.20.8, and the
  exterior-zero results for 𝑸.
- Struve: 11.6.3, 11.6.4, 11.11.6, 11.11.14–19 (11.11.18–19 by cubic stationary phase).
- Continued fractions: 6.9.1, 7.9.1–3, 7.18.13, 8.9.1, 8.10.5, 13.5.1, 13.5.3, 13.17.1, 13.17.3,
  15.7.1, 15.7.3, 15.7.5, with stronger-than-DLMF forms of 13.5.1 and 13.5.3.
- Zeros: 13.9.1, 13.9.4, 13.9.5, 13.9.6 (corrected), 13.9.9; Ei, Ci and si zeros (6.13); the real
  zeros of γ*(a, x) and 8.13.1.
- Integral representations: 7.7.2, 7.7.10, 7.7.11, 7.14.4 and companion, 8.6.10, 8.6.12, 8.19.26,
  12.5.2, 12.5.3, 12.5.8, 12.5.9 and their continuation across poles, 12.12.1, 12.12.2.
- Parabolic cylinder uniform expansions: the U and U′ parts of 12.10.3–4 with one normalizer
  (`pcf_negative_parameter_uniform_U_UDeriv`).

### Statement found false (14.14.1 corrected 2026-10-21)

- DLMF 14.14.1 (`associatedP_continued_fraction`, `DLMF/Legendre/Series.lean`): the statement
  matches DLMF, but the continued fraction does not converge to ½(x²−1)^{1/2} P^μ_ν(x)/P^{μ−1}_ν(x).
  P^μ_ν is a dominant solution of the order recurrence 14.10.6, and the fraction converges to the
  ratio of the minimal solution w(μ) = Γ(ν+μ+1)P^{−μ}_ν(x)/Γ(ν−μ+1), that is, to
  ½(x²−1)^{1/2}(ν+μ)(ν−μ+1)P^{−μ}_ν(x)/P^{1−μ}_ν(x). At (ν, μ, x) = (0.3, 0.4, 1.5) the fraction
  is 0.345161187628545…, the printed right side 0.812720708269563…, and the minimal-solution ratio
  agrees with the fraction to 30 digits; likewise at (1.7, 0.2, 3) and (2.2, 1.3, 1.2), and to 15
  digits at four further points with complex ν, μ. DLMF erratum. Corrected with the user's
  agreement: the statement now has the value ½(x²−1)^{1/2}(ν+μ)(ν−μ+1)P^{−μ}_ν(x)/P^{1−μ}_ν(x)
  and the hypothesis P^{1−μ}_ν(x) ≠ 0 in place of P^{μ−1}_ν(x) ≠ 0. Still admitted.
- `struveH_barnes` (11.5.8): a first quadrature check disagreed (−5.7947+0.2007i against
  −5.8814+0.1482i), probably because of slow algebraic decay; unverified, not judged false.

### Remaining blockers identified by the agents

- Global zero counts on large contours (argument principle) for the erf/Fresnel, Airy Bi, Kummer U
  sector zeros; all-order inversion of zero expansions; Sturm oscillation counts for Ferrers
  functions and the Scorer functions.
- Continued fractions with complex parameters (8.9.2, 8.19.17: parabola theorem or
  Stieltjes–Vitali); large-parameter ₂F₁ bounds up to x < 1 (8.17.22); 14.14.3 (the sum
  criterion).
- Full-order Laplace and Watson expansions with parameter-dependent phases and coefficient
  identification (11.6.6–7, 11.11.8–13); Debye asymptotics of I_ν(λν) for complex ν (11.6.9).
- Hankel loop contours (12.5.5), Fourier inversion on vertical lines (12.5.6–7), principal-value
  integrals (8.6.9, 9.10.19, 9.12.23), Stieltjes transforms (9.10.18, 9.12.22), Mellin–Barnes for
  1/(1+t²) with complex scale (7.7.13–14).
- Legendre: Mehler–Dirichlet and Laplace-type integrals (14.12, 14.25), Mehler–Fock (14.20.13–14),
  addition theorems (14.18), Abel boundary convergence (14.13), Gaunt (14.17.17).
- Turning-point theory for the V parts of 12.10.3–6 and for 14.15.

## Rename to lean-DLMF (2026-10-21)

The project is renamed from lean-AAR to lean-DLMF: the DLMF chapters are now the organizing
principle, with AAR, Temme, Beals–Wong and Duverney as supporting sources.

- Files: `AAR/` → `DLMF/`, `AAR.lean` → `DLMF.lean`; the former `AAR/DLMF/` (Framework,
  MathlibCoverage, Upstream, UpstreamElementary) → `DLMF/Core/`.
- Modules and namespaces `AAR.X` → `DLMF.X`; the former namespace `AAR.DLMF` is merged into the
  root namespace `DLMF`. No declaration names clashed.
- `lakefile.toml`: build directory `.lake/build-DLMF`, library roots `DLMF` and `ToMathlib`.
- Scripts (`DumpDLMF.lean`, `dlmf_ledgers.py`, `dlmf_index_page.py`, the page template) use the
  project key `lean-DLMF`.
- In the documentation "AAR" now denotes the Andrews–Askey–Roy book. Dated records above keep
  the names, paths and scratch directories in use at the time.

### Validation

- The ordinary build passes (9,959 jobs) with no errors; the source census finds 391 admissions.
- The compiled audit covers 10,967 `DLMF`/`ToMathlib` theorems; the 391 depending on `sorryAx`
  are exactly the pre-rename list under the new names, and there is no other non-standard axiom.
- The DLMF dump is unchanged (3,070 records: 2,732 proved, 338 admitted, 178 pointers); the
  acquisition ledgers were regenerated from it.
- The mirrored `ToMathlib` files `Analysis/Integral/Laplace.lean` and
  `Analysis/Complex/Carlson.lean` still match `../lean-codes`.
- No commit was made.

## Round 8 (2026-10-21)

Before the round, at the user's request, the two numerical accuracy claims of DLMF 11.15(ii) were
removed as excluded: numerical: `struveK_rational_approximation` (admitted) and
`struveH_polynomial_approximation` (proved, by the Weierstrass theorem). AGENTS.md now records
that DLMF statements have priority over AAR-only results, which are kept at lower priority.
`struveH_barnes` (11.5.8) keeps its uncertain status, now also noted in its docstring.

Six parallel agents with file-level ownership, four of them sent back for a second pass;
390 → 332 (58 admissions proved, none added). No statement was changed except the user-approved
9.7.17 correction below.

Removals by area: Legendre −20 (89 → 69), error functions −11, Airy −9, incomplete Gamma −6,
parabolic cylinder −6, spherical harmonics −2, exponential integrals −3, orthogonal
polynomials −1.

New modules:

- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Calculus/DoubleZeroSqrt.lean`: the Airy-type turning-point coordinate ζ
  ((2/3)ζ^{3/2} = ∫√p on each side of a simple zero of p): closed forms, analyticity,
  continuity, ζ'(x₀) = p'(x₀)^{1/3}, analytic inverse; signed square roots at a double zero.
- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Complex/MellinBarnesSeries.lean`: closing a vertical contour across
  infinitely many simple poles (`mbSeries_tendsto`, partial-sum limit; `mbSeries_hasSum`), with the
  Gamma residue factorization.
- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Integral/PrincipalValue.lean`: symmetric-excision principal values,
  existence for f/(t−c) with f differentiable at c, and the log-term formula.
- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/ODE/SturmCount.lean`: maximum principles (also for inhomogeneous equations),
  oscillation at ±∞, Rolle for infinitely many zeros, interlacing from a signed Wronskian, zero
  counts over a partition.
- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/ODE/SturmDeformation.lean`: with simple zeros and nonvanishing endpoints the
  zero count is locally constant, hence constant on preconnected parameter sets; half-line version
  with a uniform zero-free region.
- DLMF: `Airy/ErrorBoundTools.lean` (uniform bounds for Ai, Ai′ on the closed plane, well
  conditioning), `Bessel/SphericalSeriesTools.lean` (telescoping principle for spherical-Bessel
  series, with Euler-operator variant and coefficient bounds), `SphericalHarmonics/AdditionTools.lean`.
- Import change: `Airy/Asymptotics.lean` no longer imports `Airy/Equation.lean` (9.2.14–15 are
  re-proved privately); `Equation.lean` imports `ErrorBoundTools.lean`.

Main results proved:

- Uniform-asymptotics coordinates: `pcfInverseZeta_spec`, `pcfZeta_analytic_at_one`,
  `pcfAiryChi_formula` (12.10), `conicalRho_mapping`, `conicalEta_spec`, `conicalEta_mapping`
  (14.20), `legendreOrderEta_spec`, `legendreOrderEta_bijective`, `legendreCylinderZeta_spec` (14.15).
- Airy: the four well-conditioned pairs of Table 9.2.1 (`Airy/Equation.lean` now admission-free).
- Zeros: Scorer 9.12(xi) (Gi has no zeros on [0, ∞), Gi′ exactly one; infinitely many negative
  zeros of Gi and Gi′), 12.11(i) `pcfU_positive_zero_count` by parameter deformation from the
  Hermite case, `pcfU_hermite_zero_count`, `pcfV_no_positive_zero`, `ferrersPQ_interlace`,
  `ferrersQ_nat_zero_count`.
- Principal values and integrals: 6.2.8, 8.6.2, 8.6.9 (convergence), 9.10.19 (convergence).
- Series: 6.10.4, 6.10.8, 7.6.8, 7.6.10, 7.6.11, 8.7.4, 8.7.5 (all a, by continuity at a ∈ −ℕ),
  8.21.16, 8.21.17, and the absolute/non-absolute convergence statements of 14.13.
- Error-function bounds 7.8.2–7.8.8 and the monotonicity of the normalized Dawson integral
  (`ErrorFunctions/BoundsAsymptotics.lean` now admission-free).
- Spherical harmonics and addition: the Gegenbauer addition theorem `ultrasphPoly_addition`
  (18.18.8; induction on n with varying λ), the product integral 18.17.5,
  `harmonicHomogeneous_orthogonal` (Gaussian weight and integration by parts), the spherical
  harmonic Rodrigues formula, orthonormality, Herglotz generating function, `scaledOblate_real`,
  and 14.5.22–24, 14.5.26 (`Legendre/SpecialValues.lean` and `Legendre/Harmonics.lean` now
  admission-free).

### Statement correction (9.7.17, user-approved 2026-10-21)

`airyErrorMultiplier` (`DLMF/Airy/Asymptotics.lean`) wrote DLMF's min(|csc(ph ζ)|, χ(n+σ)+1)
as `min |(sin (3 arg z/2))⁻¹| (χ+1)`. On the ray |arg z| = 2π/3, sin(ph ζ) = 0 and Lean's
`0⁻¹ = 0` made the multiplier 0, so `airy_ai_complex_error` and `airy_aiDeriv_complex_error`
asserted ‖R_N(z)‖ ≤ 0 (refuted in Lean at z = e^{2πi/3}, and numerically). Transcription defect,
not a DLMF erratum. The case sin = 0 now gives χ(n+σ)+1. The endpoint |arg z| = π, where
cos(ph ζ) = 0, is excluded by the existing hypothesis `z ∈ slitPlane`, as in DLMF (|ph z| < π).
The two counterexample theorems were removed; both statements remain admitted.

### Statements found false (corrected 2026-10-22, user-approved; still admitted)

- `legendreDegreeY_spec` (14.15.21–22, `DLMF/Legendre/UniformCoordinates.lean`): for
  x ≥ √(1−α²) the relation `LegendreYRelation` asks only y ≤ α², but the equation has a root in
  (0, α²) and one in (−∞, 0) whenever its right side is negative (α = 0.6: x = 0.9 gives
  y = 0.177237 and y = −0.116411; x = 0.81 gives 0.341463 and −0.157066; checked with mpmath).
  DLMF's y decreases through 0 at x = 1, so the intended root has y > 0 for x < 1 and y < 0 for
  x > 1. Corrected: the relation now has (x < 1 → 0 < y) ∧ (1 < x → y < 0) in that case.
  Transcription defect, not a DLMF erratum. `legendreDegreeY_monotone` depends on it.
- `incompleteGamma_increment_series` (8.7.2, `DLMF/IncompleteGamma/SeriesRecurrences.lean`): the
  hypotheses x, x+y ∈ slitPlane and |y| < |x| allow the segment [x, x+y] to cross the cut of the
  principal (x+y)^a. At a = 0.3, x = −1+0.1i, y = −0.2i the left side is −6.7644i and the
  series −0.43925+0.31913i; the identity holds to 30 digits when the segment stays in the cut
  plane (x = −1+0.1i, y = 0.05i; x = 1+0.5i, y = 0.3+0.2i). Corrected: the hypothesis
  x + y ∈ slitPlane is replaced by x + t y ∈ slitPlane for all t ∈ [0, 1] (the series is the
  continuation of γ(a, ·) from x along the segment). A branch-convention defect of the
  formalization: DLMF states 8.7.2 for multivalued functions with |y| < |x|.

### Remaining blockers identified by the agents

- Legendre 14.16 zero counts: the deformation tool applies; missing joint continuity of the
  Ferrers functions in (ν, x), control near ±1, and base counts. Kummer U counts: continuity in
  a. Scorer interlacing: a certified bound on the first negative hump of Gi.
- Airy 9.7.16 needs the corrected 9.7.17 (Laplace remainder on ph ζ = ±π); 9.7.23 needs
  exponentially improved bounds; modulus–phase error bounds need signed bounds for 1/M², x/N²;
  generalized Airy needs K_ν asymptotics in sectors wider than π/2.
- Mellin–Barnes: Stirling-type ratio bounds uniform along shifted lines; the Mellin transform of
  K_ν (10.43.19); Plemelj boundary limits for the Stieltjes targets.
- Uniform-expansion coefficients: explicit Taylor coefficients of inverse coordinates, removable
  singularities of Olver's coefficients at ζ = 0, a global inverse for the incomplete-Gamma η.
- Legendre/Ferrers addition theorems for complex degree need continuation in ν; spherical-harmonic
  addition and Funk–Hecke need the dimension and spanning theory; 14.13 values need ₂F₁ on
  |ζ| = 1; Jacobi generating functions need growth bounds for complex α, β.

## Round 9 statement correction (2026-10-22, user-approved)

- `cylinderEnvelopeCross_spec` (14.15.23, `DLMF/Legendre/UniformCoordinates.lean`): DLMF defines
  X_c as the largest positive root of U(−c, x) = Ū(−c, x) for c ≥ 0. At c = 0 the only root is
  x = 0 (U(0,0) = Ū(0,0); mpmath gives U − Ū = −0.0233, −0.349, −1.177 at x = 0.02, 0.3, 1 and no
  sign change on (0, 6]), so the asserted `0 < cylinderEnvelopeCross 0` was false; the project's
  definition (supremum of the positive roots) gives X₀ = 0, for which the envelope formula still
  makes sense. For c > 0 a positive root exists (≈ 0.034 at c = 0.01, ≈ 0.288 at c = 0.1).
  Corrected: the first conjunct is now `0 < c → 0 < cylinderEnvelopeCross c`; the root equation and
  the absence of roots beyond X_c are kept for all c ≥ 0. A degenerate case of DLMF's definition.
  Still admitted.

## Round 9 DLMF errata in §14.16 (2026-10-22, user-approved)

Notation 14.16.1: μ = m + δμ, ν = n + δν. All three statements matched DLMF as printed; checked
with mpmath (`legenp`/`legenq`, sign changes on a fine grid) by the round-9 agent and independently.
All three remain admitted.

- 14.16(ii), zeros of 𝖯^μ_ν (`ferrersPExtraZero`, used by `ferrersP_zero_count`): DLMF lists
  max(⌈ν−|μ|⌉, 0) zeros for μ > 0, n ≥ m, δν > δμ and one extra zero for μ > 0, n > m, δν ≤ δμ,
  so the case n = m, δν ≤ δμ is not covered. It has the extra zero: (ν, μ) = (1/4, 3/4),
  (0.452, 0.939), (1.2, 1.7), (2.1, 2.9), (0.3, 0.3), (1.6, 1.6) each give one zero against a
  base count 0; the neighbouring cases (2.6, 1.3), (2.3, 1.6), (0.4, 2.7), (0.4, 1.7) agree with
  DLMF. Corrected: the first extra-zero case reads n ≥ m (`⌊μ⌋ ≤ ⌊ν⌋`).
- 14.16(ii), zeros of 𝖰^μ_ν (`ferrersQ_zero_count_range`): the parity rule (even when cos νπ and
  cos μπ have opposite signs, odd when the same) fails for μ < 0: at ν = −0.369, μ = −0.206 both
  cosines are positive, but 𝖰^μ_ν > 0 on (−1, 1) (values 2.63 to 7.99); also (0.443, −1.707) and
  (0.156, −2.609). No failure in 40 samples with μ > 0. Corrected: the parity clauses are asserted
  for 0 ≤ μ; the count range max(⌈ν−|μ|⌉, 0) + k, k ∈ {−1, 0, 1, 2}, is kept for all μ.
- 14.16(iii), zeros of P^μ_ν on (1, ∞) (`associatedPOneZero`, used by `associatedP_zero_count`):
  case (b) (μ ≤ ν, μ non-integer, ⌊μ⌋ odd) fails for μ < 0. Near x = 1, P^μ_ν behaves like
  ((x+1)/(x−1))^{μ/2}/Γ(1−μ), and the leading term at ∞ has the factor 1/Γ(ν−μ+1); both are
  positive for μ < 0 ≤ ν + 1/2, and P^μ_ν > 0 on (1, ∞) at ν = 0.184, μ = −0.088 (0.676 just
  above 1, 150.6 at x = 1 + 10¹²); 12 failures in 50 samples, all with μ < 0. Corrected: case (b)
  requires μ > 0.

## Round 9 (2026-10-22)

Six parallel agents with file-level ownership, four of them sent back for a second pass;
332 → 306 (26 admissions proved, none added). Statement changes were limited to the
user-approved corrections recorded above (14.15.23 and the three §14.16 errata) and in round 8
(14.15.21–22, 8.7.2).

Removals by area: Legendre −8, Airy −4, error functions −3, exponential integrals −3, orthogonal
polynomials −3, spherical harmonics −2, incomplete Gamma −1, parabolic cylinder −1, Struve −1.

New modules:

- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Asymptotics/StieltjesRemainder.lean`: Stieltjes transforms
  ∫φ(τ)/(1+τ/S) dτ with nonnegative density: exact remainder, bounds by the first neglected term
  with factor 1 (Re S ≥ 0) and |csc arg S|, signed enclosures on the imaginary axis, and the
  χ-type bounds needed beyond |ph S| = π/2.
- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Calculus/InverseSeries.lean`: checking a candidate inverse expansion by
  substitution (left inverses with a strict derivative), roots of algebraic relations in big-O form.
- DLMF: `Airy/StieltjesForm.lean` (ray-Laplace model of the Airy remainder, the χ integral
  Γ(m+1)∫(W²+ρ²)^{−(m+1)/2} = Γ(m)χ(m)W^{−m}), `Bessel/KMellin.lean` (10.43.19, new tag),
  `Bessel/SphericalProductTools.lean` (Σ(2n+1)jₙ² = 1, Σ(−1)ⁿ(2n+1)jₙ(x)jₙ(y) = j₀(x+y)),
  `Gamma/ShiftedLineBounds.lean` (Gamma bounds uniform along shifted vertical lines),
  `Legendre/ZeroTools.lean` (Sturm comparison on (−1, 1) with endpoint control; zero count of
  𝖯^{−m}_ν), `SphericalHarmonics/ZonalTools.lean` (harmonic polynomials as `HarmonicOnNhd`, the
  Poisson-kernel expansion, the reproducing property of zonal polynomials).

Main results proved:

- Airy: 9.7.17 for Ai in all four sectors (`airy_ai_complex_error`, with a sharp Gamma inequality
  (N/(2e))^N ≤ √(2π(N+1/6))·u_N), and 9.7.16 for Bi (`airy_bi_real_error`, `airy_bi_bound`);
  9.10.17 (`airyAi_mellin`).
- Legendre: the 14.16 zero counts `ferrersP_nonpositive_order_zero_count`,
  `ferrersP_nat_degree_zero_count`, `ferrersP_positive_integer_order_zero_count`; 14.15.21–22
  (`legendreDegreeY_spec`, `legendreDegreeY_monotone`); `besselEnvelopeCross_spec`; the Heine series
  14.28.2 (`legendre_heine_series`, `legendre_heine_uniform`).
- Spherical harmonics: the addition theorem and Funk–Hecke (`spherical_addition`, `funk_hecke`);
  `SphericalHarmonics/Theorems.lean` is admission-free.
- Orthogonal polynomials: 18.10.1, 18.10.2, 18.17.21 (`OrthogonalPolynomials/Integrals.lean`
  admission-free).
- Series: 8.7.2 (corrected), 7.6.9 (stronger than DLMF: all complex b), 6.10.1, 6.10.5, 6.10.6
  (`ExpIntegrals/Expansions.lean` and `ErrorFunctions/Series.lean` admission-free).
- Mellin–Barnes: 7.7.13, 7.7.14 (`ErrorFunctions/Integrals.lean` admission-free), 11.5.9.
- Parabolic cylinder: 12.10.41 (`pcfInverseZeta_taylor`).

### Remaining blockers identified by the agents

- Airy: a Stieltjes form for Ai′ with σ = 0 (9.7.17 for Ai′, 9.7.16 for Bi′); the negative-axis

proved, none added). No statement was changed. One declaration was moved verbatim (rule agreed for

`DLMF/SphericalHarmonics/BesselAddition.lean`, because the Gegenbauer theory it needs lies
downstream of `Bessel/Theorems.lean`; no file used it.

Removals by area: Legendre −5, Confluent −3, orthogonal polynomials −3, Bessel −1, Airy −1.

New modules:

- `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Complex/LagrangeBurmann.lean`: the Lagrange–Bürmann formula at the
  coefficient level, Σ zⁿ (FΦⁿ)⁽ⁿ⁾(0)/n! = F(w)/g(w) when t − zΦ(t) = (t − w)g(t) on a circle
  (Cauchy formula, no argument principle), and extension of a power-series identity from a small
  disc to the disc of holomorphy.
- DLMF: `Confluent/ZeroTools.lean` (Sturm comparison on (0, ∞) with a general weight; U real and
  in self-adjoint form; the exact count of positive zeros of U(a, b, ·); U on the negative axis:
  connection formula, Wronskian, no common zeros with 𝐌), `Legendre/ZeroCounts.lean` (count bounds
  and parity by Sturm separation; reflection Wronskians W{𝖯^{−μ}_ν(−x), 𝖯^μ_ν} and
  W{𝖯^{−μ}_ν(−x), 𝖰^μ_ν}), `Legendre/AdditionTools.lean` (cosine series of even C² periodic
  functions, a Carlson wrapper on ℂ, Laplace-type growth bounds in ν, the sharp bound

  tagged 16.13.4, and Bailey's product formula ₂F₁(a,b;c;X)₂F₁(a,b;c′;Y) = F4(a,b;c,c′;X(1−Y),Y(1−X))
  for c + c′ = a + b + 1, near 0), `SphericalHarmonics/BesselAddition.lean`.

Main results proved:

- Bessel: Gegenbauer's addition theorem 10.23.8 for complex ν, Re ν > 0, by a direct power-series
  argument (Taylor shift of ₀F̃₁, the monomial expansion 18.18.17, a ₀F₁ product formula).
- Kummer: 13.9.13 (no zeros of U on the negative axis, all cases), 13.9.14 (positive zeros), and

## `struveH_barnes` (11.5.8) numerical check (2026-10-22)

The round-7 discrepancy was quadrature truncation. A 30-digit mpmath check of the statement as
written (whole-line integral in y of the integrand at s = c + iy against −2π(x/2)^{−ν−1}H_ν(x)),
integrating over [−Y, Y] with unit subintervals, gives:

- ν = 3, x = 1.5, c = −0.3 (decay |y|^{−4.4}): Y = 800 agrees to 4·10⁻¹⁴.
- ν = 2.5 + 0.7i, x = 2, c = −0.4 (decay |y|^{−3.7}): Y = 800 agrees to 4·10⁻¹².
- ν = 1 − i, x = 0.8, c = −0.2 (decay |y|^{−2.6}): error 5·10⁻⁷ at Y = 200, 1.3·10⁻⁸ at Y = 800.
- ν = 0.5, x = 3, c = −0.5 (decay |y|^{−1.5}): error 4.6·10⁻⁵ at Y = 200, 4.4·10⁻⁶ at Y = 800.

The errors shrink with Y as the algebraic tails predict. The sign and normalization also follow
from the residues (−1)^k of π/sin(πs) at s = k ≥ 0 and the clockwise closing of the contour to the
right. The statement is judged correct and is released for proof in round 11.

## Duplication and reuse review (2026-10-22)

### Checked starting status

The checkout has advanced beyond the round-13 totals above. The initial ordinary build passes
with 10,039 jobs. There are 419 Lean source modules under `DLMF/` and `ToMathlib/`, about
280,600 lines, and 207 explicit source admissions after excluding comments. A compiled
`Lean.collectAxioms` audit finds 12,948 project theorems, of which 213 depend on `sorryAx`;
there are no other nonstandard axioms. The six additional dependences are why the source
and compiled counts differ. This is a proof-maintenance pass, not a new admission-proof round.

The largest source-admission groups are incomplete Gamma (42), Airy (37), Legendre (34),
orthogonal polynomials (30), parabolic cylinder (22), Struve (12), and Confluent (9).
Earlier numerical totals in this document and the README are historical snapshots.

### Consolidation performed

- **Scalar power series:** move the shared uniqueness and Cauchy-product proofs to
  `DLMFMathlib/Analysis/Complex/ScalarSeries.lean`, in namespace `Complex`. Saalschütz now uses
  them directly. The old public orthogonal-polynomial and hypergeometric interfaces are small
  adapters. The new module imports Mathlib only.
- **Pochhammer algebra:** share reflection and binomial convolution in
  `Hypergeometric/EulerIntegral.lean`, already imported by both Appell F4 and the classical
  polynomial library. Both families retain their public names as adapters. The weighted Jacobi
  generating-function proof reuses Appell's Pochhammer norm bound, the existing duplication
  identity, and the earlier antidiagonal summation lemma. Continuation reuses the nonnegativity
  and monotonicity results in `Hypergeometric/Series.lean`; Saalschütz reuses its binomial series.
- **Legendre from general Gauss theory:** add
  `DLMF.Hypergeometric.tendsto_regularizedGaussHG_left_one` in `Hypergeometric/Boundary.lean`.
  It applies Mathlib's Abel limit theorem to the existing regularized Gauss summation theorem.
  Both Legendre boundary-limit helpers become adapters, eliminating a second exceptional-
  parameter calculation. All denominator parameters are retained. The separately proved
  Euler-transformed associated-P formula now invokes `associatedP_neg_euler`; two repeated
  branch-power/asymptotic helpers reuse `BoundaryAsymptotics`.
- **Integral holomorphy:** replace the two 57-line proofs in Gamma's `BetaFunction.lean` and
  `Selberg/Basic.lean` by applications of the more general existing
  `Complex.differentiableOn_integral_of_locally_dominated`. Closed-ball domination supplies
  the neighborhood required by that theorem. No extra measurability or integrability
  assumptions are added.
- **Bessel:** move `besselK_neg`, including its DLMF 10.27.3 tag, verbatim from `Elementary`
  to `SecondKind`. The integral representation can now call it directly, so its private copy
  disappears. Importing `Elementary` still provides the original theorem.
- **Jacobi:** move `jacobiPolyP_one_ne_zero` from `Differential` to `Explicit`, replacing the
  private earlier copy and updating its callers. The public name and hypotheses are retained.
- **Airy:** reuse the rotation connection formulas, sector-rotation limit, real-axis
  derivative facts, and derivative sign at zero instead of reproving them in later modules.
- **Spherical harmonics:** both homogeneous-scaling helpers now invoke TauCeti's existing
  `MvPolynomial.IsHomogeneous.eval₂_const_mul`; the public Poisson reproduction theorem
  invokes `zonal_poisson` instead of repeating its continuous-extension and uniqueness proof.

No existing public theorem statement, branch convention, normalization, exceptional-parameter
case, or convergence hypothesis was changed. Small public adapters deliberately remain where
they preserve useful names and DLMF-facing interfaces.

### What the scan does and does not establish

The review combines a whole-source scan for repeated theorem signatures with manual inspection
of proof bodies and the import graph. Many matching signatures are already a helper followed
by a one-line DLMF-tagged theorem; those are not duplicate proofs. This is not an exhaustive
semantic-equivalence check of all 280,600 lines.

The named-function interfaces already reuse substantial general theory: `Legendre/Basic`
derives its differential equation from the regularized Gauss equation; `Legendre/Recurrences`
uses contiguous relations; `Legendre/RelatedFunctions` applies Euler transformations for
Gegenbauer and Jacobi representations. The remaining asymptotic, zero-distribution and
transform admissions generally need analytic estimates beyond identifying a hypergeometric
special case. No admission was counted as resolved merely by invoking an admitted result.

Further concrete consolidation candidates remain:

- The logarithmic and Euler-constant integrals in `Airy/ScorerAsymptotics.lean`
  (`scorerPrim_integral_log`, `scorerPrim_integral_euler`) are copied in
  `Struve/Asymptotics.lean` (`struvePrim_integral_log`, `struvePrim_integral_euler`). A shared
  integral module would avoid importing one special-function chapter into the other.
- Uniform vertical Gamma bounds and the Gamma-ratio norm comparison in
  `ParabolicCylinder/BarnesContinuation.lean` (`pcfBC_Gamma_bound`,
  `pcfBC_Gamma_bound_pt`, `pcfBC_norm_Gamma_le_ratio`) repeat the corresponding `incRep_`
  proofs in `IncompleteGamma/Representations.lean`. These belong in a shared Gamma/Barnes
  layer, with the existing Barnes-residue dependencies checked before moving them.
- Gamma third-argument reflection constants and complex trigonometric growth estimates recur
  in Airy, hypergeometric, Legendre, Struve and parabolic-cylinder proofs. Consolidate them
  below those chapters rather than adding cross-chapter dependencies solely for a helper.

Two important non-duplicates must remain distinguished: totalized ordinary `₂F₁` series versus
`regularizedGaussHG` on the cut plane, and principal-sheet special functions versus their
explicit logarithmic-plane continuations. Similarly, the hypergeometric definitions of Jacobi
and Gegenbauer polynomials have exceptional-parameter behavior which cannot be erased by
cancelling a possibly zero Pochhammer or Gamma factor. The Airy and core principal-value
predicates also have different arguments and convergence conditions; similar names do not
justify replacing one by the other.

### Validation and final status

- The final ordinary command `lake build > /tmp/DLMF-build.log 2>&1` passes (10,043 jobs).
- All 29 changed Lean files pass
  `lake env lean -DautoImplicit=false -DrelaxedAutoImplicit=false path/to/File.lean`
